> Historical redmine documentation saved before the main port. Paths/line numbers describe that earlier snapshot; use ../MEMORY_MAP.md for active code.

# Function registration: added-line ledger

Read [the walkthrough](FUNCTION_REGISTRATION_WALKTHROUGH.md) first. This ledger shows every nonblank added/replaced production, test and verification-tool line for this feature, with current source line numbers. Existing user changes to `.classpath` and the parser experiment `main` are excluded. Braces close the preceding block; continuation rows explain the whole statement they continue.

The request builder's old eager parser creation and builder parser field were removed: compilation now creates a fresh parser after the factory registry is known. The compiler's old first-result/no-argument instantiation was replaced by entry-class selection and registry injection.
